# IMPERIA COLD WAR — Kissinger statecraft and contested regional politics.
COLDWAR_USA_KS_refresh_fiscal_host = {
	set_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_security_quote = gdp_total }
	multiply_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_security_quote = 2 }
	divide_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_security_quote = 100 }
	set_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_investment_quote = gdp_total }
	multiply_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_investment_quote = 1 }
	divide_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_investment_quote = 100 }
	set_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_settlement_quote = gdp_total }
	multiply_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_settlement_quote = 10 }
	divide_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_settlement_quote = 100 }
	set_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_reform_quote_1 = gdp_total }
	multiply_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_reform_quote_1 = 10 }
	divide_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_reform_quote_1 = 100 }
	set_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_reform_quote_2 = gdp_total }
	multiply_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_reform_quote_2 = 12 }
	divide_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_reform_quote_2 = 100 }
	set_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_reform_quote_3 = gdp_total }
	multiply_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_reform_quote_3 = 15 }
	divide_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_reform_quote_3 = 100 }
	set_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_reform_quote_4 = gdp_total }
	multiply_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_reform_quote_4 = 18 }
	divide_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_reform_quote_4 = 100 }
	set_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_emergency_quote = gdp_total }
	multiply_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_emergency_quote = 2 }
	divide_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_emergency_quote = 100 }
	set_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_repression_quote = gdp_total }
	multiply_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_repression_quote = 4 }
	divide_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_repression_quote = 100 }
	set_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_week_security = gdp_total }
	multiply_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_week_security = 0.004 }
	divide_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_week_security = 100 }
	set_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_week_investment = gdp_total }
	multiply_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_week_investment = 0.002 }
	divide_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_week_investment = 100 }
	set_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_week_rights = gdp_total }
	multiply_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_week_rights = 0.001 }
	divide_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_week_rights = 100 }
}

COLDWAR_USA_KS_refresh_fiscal = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = USA
		}
		COLDWAR_USA_KS_refresh_fiscal_usa = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = { tag = IRQ tag = PER tag = TUR tag = SYR }
		}
		COLDWAR_USA_KS_refresh_fiscal_host = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = SOV
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_week_deal_moscow_partner = gdp_total }
		multiply_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_week_deal_moscow_partner = 0.00002 }
		divide_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_week_deal_moscow_partner = 100 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = CHL
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_investment_quote = gdp_total }
		multiply_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_investment_quote = 1 }
		divide_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_investment_quote = 100 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = { tag = PER tag = TUR tag = KUR tag = PUK tag = KPI tag = KPT tag = KPS }
		}
		USA = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_refresh_fiscal_usa = yes }
	}
}

COLDWAR_USA_KS_deal_beijing_preview = {
	effect_tooltip = {
		custom_effect_tooltip = COLDWAR_USA_KS_if_accept_tt
		USA = { add_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_deal_beijing_usa
		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_preview_foreign_influence_modifier = 0.05 tooltip = COLDWAR_USA_KS_mod_foreign_influence_modifier_tt }
		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_preview_decryption_factor = 0.05 tooltip = COLDWAR_USA_KS_mod_decryption_factor_tt }
		 }
		CHI = { add_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_deal_beijing_partner
		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_preview_encryption_factor = 0.05 tooltip = COLDWAR_USA_KS_mod_encryption_factor_tt }
		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_preview_agency_upgrade_time = -0.05 tooltip = COLDWAR_USA_KS_mod_agency_upgrade_time_tt } }
	}
}

COLDWAR_USA_KS_deal_beijing_offer = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_USA_KS_deal_beijing_can_offer = yes
		}
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_deal_beijing_authorised
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_deal_beijing_pending
		CHI = {
			set_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_deal_beijing_pending
			country_event = { id = COLDWAR_USA_KS.1 }
		}
	}
}

COLDWAR_USA_KS_deal_beijing_accept = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_USA_KS_deal_beijing_accept_valid = yes
		}
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_deal_beijing_signed
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_deal_beijing_pending
		USA = {
			set_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_deal_beijing_signed
			clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_deal_beijing_pending
		}
		USA = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_reconcile_deals = yes }
	}
}

COLDWAR_USA_KS_deal_beijing_end = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = { tag = USA tag = CHI }
		}
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_deal_beijing_pending
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_deal_beijing_signed
		USA = {
			clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_deal_beijing_pending
			clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_deal_beijing_signed
			set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_deal_beijing_cooldown value = 1 days = 60 }
			remove_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_deal_beijing_usa
		}
		CHI = {
			clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_deal_beijing_pending
			clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_deal_beijing_signed
			remove_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_deal_beijing_partner
		}
	}
}

COLDWAR_USA_KS_reconcile_deals = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = USA
			IMP_is_1970_scenario = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				COLDWAR_USA_KS_deal_beijing_active = yes
			}
			if = { limit = { NOT = { has_idea = COLDWAR_USA_KS_deal_beijing_usa } } add_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_deal_beijing_usa }
			CHI = { if = { limit = { NOT = { has_idea = COLDWAR_USA_KS_deal_beijing_partner } } add_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_deal_beijing_partner } }
		}
		else = {
			remove_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_deal_beijing_usa
			CHI = { remove_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_deal_beijing_partner }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				COLDWAR_USA_KS_deal_irq_transit_active = yes
			}
			if = { limit = { NOT = { has_idea = COLDWAR_USA_KS_deal_irq_transit_usa } } add_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_deal_irq_transit_usa }
			PER = { if = { limit = { NOT = { has_idea = COLDWAR_USA_KS_deal_irq_transit_partner } } add_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_deal_irq_transit_partner } }
		}
		else = {
			remove_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_deal_irq_transit_usa
			PER = { remove_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_deal_irq_transit_partner }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				COLDWAR_USA_KS_deal_irq_anatolia_active = yes
			}
			if = { limit = { NOT = { has_idea = COLDWAR_USA_KS_deal_irq_anatolia_usa } } add_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_deal_irq_anatolia_usa }
			TUR = { if = { limit = { NOT = { has_idea = COLDWAR_USA_KS_deal_irq_anatolia_partner } } add_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_deal_irq_anatolia_partner } }
		}
		else = {
			remove_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_deal_irq_anatolia_usa
			TUR = { remove_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_deal_irq_anatolia_partner }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				COLDWAR_USA_KS_deal_tur_alliance_active = yes
			}
			if = { limit = { NOT = { has_idea = COLDWAR_USA_KS_deal_tur_alliance_usa } } add_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_deal_tur_alliance_usa }
			TUR = { if = { limit = { NOT = { has_idea = COLDWAR_USA_KS_deal_tur_alliance_partner } } add_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_deal_tur_alliance_partner } }
		}
		else = {
			remove_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_deal_tur_alliance_usa
			TUR = { remove_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_deal_tur_alliance_partner }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				COLDWAR_USA_KS_deal_irq_settlement_active = yes
			}
			if = { limit = { NOT = { has_idea = COLDWAR_USA_KS_deal_irq_settlement_usa } } add_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_deal_irq_settlement_usa }
			IRQ = { if = { limit = { NOT = { has_idea = COLDWAR_USA_KS_deal_irq_settlement_partner } } add_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_deal_irq_settlement_partner } }
		}
		else = {
			remove_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_deal_irq_settlement_usa
			IRQ = { remove_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_deal_irq_settlement_partner }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				COLDWAR_USA_KS_deal_irn_rights_active = yes
			}
			if = { limit = { NOT = { has_idea = COLDWAR_USA_KS_deal_irn_rights_usa } } add_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_deal_irn_rights_usa }
			PER = { if = { limit = { NOT = { has_idea = COLDWAR_USA_KS_deal_irn_rights_partner } } add_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_deal_irn_rights_partner } }
		}
		else = {
			remove_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_deal_irn_rights_usa
			PER = { remove_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_deal_irn_rights_partner }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				COLDWAR_USA_KS_deal_tur_rights_active = yes
			}
			if = { limit = { NOT = { has_idea = COLDWAR_USA_KS_deal_tur_rights_usa } } add_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_deal_tur_rights_usa }
			TUR = { if = { limit = { NOT = { has_idea = COLDWAR_USA_KS_deal_tur_rights_partner } } add_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_deal_tur_rights_partner } }
		}
		else = {
			remove_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_deal_tur_rights_usa
			TUR = { remove_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_deal_tur_rights_partner }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				COLDWAR_USA_KS_deal_syr_rights_active = yes
			}
			if = { limit = { NOT = { has_idea = COLDWAR_USA_KS_deal_syr_rights_usa } } add_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_deal_syr_rights_usa }
			SYR = { if = { limit = { NOT = { has_idea = COLDWAR_USA_KS_deal_syr_rights_partner } } add_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_deal_syr_rights_partner } }
		}
		else = {
			remove_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_deal_syr_rights_usa
			SYR = { remove_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_deal_syr_rights_partner }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				COLDWAR_USA_KS_deal_angola_active = yes
			}
			if = { limit = { NOT = { has_idea = COLDWAR_USA_KS_deal_angola_usa } } add_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_deal_angola_usa }
			UNI = { if = { limit = { NOT = { has_idea = COLDWAR_USA_KS_deal_angola_partner } } add_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_deal_angola_partner } }
		}
		else = {
			remove_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_deal_angola_usa
			UNI = { remove_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_deal_angola_partner }
		}
	}
}

COLDWAR_USA_KS_deal_streams = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = USA
			USA = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_deal_beijing_active = yes }
		}
		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_weekly_expense = COLDWAR_USA_KS_week_deal_beijing_usa }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = USA
			USA = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_deal_irq_transit_active = yes }
		}
		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_weekly_expense = COLDWAR_USA_KS_week_deal_irq_transit_usa }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = PER
			USA = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_deal_irq_transit_active = yes }
		}
		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_weekly_income = USA.COLDWAR_USA_KS_week_deal_irq_transit_usa }
	}

	}
	if = {

			tag = USA

		}
		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_weekly_expense = COLDWAR_USA_KS_week_deal_tur_alliance_usa }
	}
	if = {

			tag = USA
			USA = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_deal_irq_settlement_active = yes }

	}

COLDWAR_USA_KS_init_irq = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = USA
			country_exists = IRQ
		}
		IRQ = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_tracked }
				}
				set_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_tracked
				set_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_unrest = 52 }
				set_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_stabilisation = 0 }
				set_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_organisation = 0 }
				set_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_qualifying_days = 0 }
				set_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_warning_days = 0 }
				COLDWAR_USA_KS_refresh_host = yes
			}
		}
	}
}

COLDWAR_USA_KS_tick_irq = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = IRQ
			IMP_is_1970_scenario = yes
			has_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_tracked
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_drift = 0 }
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_pacified }
			}
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_drift = 1 }
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_support = 0 }
		if = {
			limit = {
				USA = { check_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_stage_irq = 3 } }
				COLDWAR_USA_KS_funding_irq = yes
				NOT = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_settlement_protects_irq = yes }
			}
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_support = 5.32 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				USA = { check_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_stage_irq = 2 } }
				COLDWAR_USA_KS_funding_irq = yes
				NOT = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_settlement_protects_irq = yes }
			}
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_support = 2.85 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				USA = { check_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_stage_irq = 1 } }
				COLDWAR_USA_KS_funding_irq = yes
				NOT = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_settlement_protects_irq = yes }
			}
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_support = 1.33 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				COLDWAR_USA_KS_pressure_irq = yes
			}
			add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_drift = 9 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_trauma_weeks > 0 }
			}
			add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_drift = COLDWAR_USA_KS_trauma_drift }
		}
		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_drift = COLDWAR_USA_KS_support }
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_organisation_effect = COLDWAR_USA_KS_organisation }
		multiply_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_organisation_effect = 0.01 }
		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_drift = COLDWAR_USA_KS_organisation_effect }
		if = {
			limit = {
				num_divisions_in_states = { count > 0 states = { 164 633 630 1078 } }
			}
			add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_drift = -1.2 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				num_divisions_in_states = { count > 1 states = { 164 633 630 1078 } }
			}
			add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_drift = -1.2 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				num_divisions_in_states = { count > 2 states = { 164 633 630 1078 } }
			}
			add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_drift = -1.2 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				num_divisions_in_states = { count > 3 states = { 164 633 630 1078 } }
			}
			add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_drift = -1.2 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_security
				COLDWAR_USA_KS_forces_irq = yes
			}
			add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_drift = -3 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_security
				COLDWAR_USA_KS_forces_irq = yes
			}
			add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_stabilisation = 3.5 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_investment
			}
			add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_stabilisation = 0.98 }
		}
		else = {
			add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_stabilisation = -0.7 }
		}
		clamp_variable = { var = COLDWAR_USA_KS_stabilisation min = 0 max = 100 }
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_legacy_effect = COLDWAR_USA_KS_stabilisation }
		multiply_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_legacy_effect = -0.08 }
		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_drift = COLDWAR_USA_KS_legacy_effect }
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_investment
			}
			add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_investment_weeks = 1 }
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_investment_weeks > 5 }
				}
				add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_drift = -2.1 }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				COLDWAR_USA_KS_rights_irq = yes
			}
			add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_drift = -4.5 }
			add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_stabilisation = 1.4 }
		}
		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_unrest = COLDWAR_USA_KS_drift }
		clamp_variable = { var = COLDWAR_USA_KS_unrest min = 0 max = 100 }
		clamp_variable = { var = COLDWAR_USA_KS_stabilisation min = 0 max = 100 }
		COLDWAR_USA_KS_peace_tick_irq = yes
		COLDWAR_USA_KS_repression_tick = yes
		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_ui_dirty = 1 }
		if = {
			limit = {
				COLDWAR_USA_KS_region_irq = yes
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_unrest > 79 }
				NOT = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_settlement_protects_irq = yes }
				COLDWAR_USA_KS_material_irq = yes
				COLDWAR_USA_KS_funding_irq = yes
				USA = { check_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_stage_irq > 2 } }
				NOT = { has_war_with = KUR }
				NOT = { has_war_with = PUK }
			}
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_below_days = 0 }
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_qualifying_days > 29 }
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_warning }
					}
					set_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_warning
					country_event = { id = COLDWAR_USA_KS.110 }
				}
				add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_warning_days = 7 }
				if = {
					limit = {
						check_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_warning_days > 44 }
					}
					set_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_mobilisation_ready
				}
			}
			else = {
				add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_qualifying_days = 7 }
			}
		}
		else = {
			add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_below_days = 7 }
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_below_days > 14 }
				}
				set_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_qualifying_days = 0 }
				set_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_warning_days = 0 }
				clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_warning
				clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_mobilisation_ready
			}
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = IRQ
			has_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_tracked
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_network_cooldown > 0 }
		}
		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_network_cooldown = -1 }
	}
}

COLDWAR_USA_KS_irq_security = {
	set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_invoice = gdp_total }
	multiply_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_invoice = 2 }
	divide_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_invoice = 100 }
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = IRQ
			has_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_tracked
			NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_security }
			COLDWAR_USA_KS_forces_irq = yes
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_security_quote > 0 }
			check_variable = { var = treasury value = COLDWAR_USA_KS_invoice compare = greater_than_or_equals }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = COLDWAR_USA_KS_invoice }
		multiply_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -1 }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_security
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_investment
		remove_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_security_programme
		remove_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_investment_programme
		add_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_security_programme
		update_money_dirty_variable = yes
	}
}

COLDWAR_USA_KS_irq_investment = {
	set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_invoice = gdp_total }
	multiply_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_invoice = 1 }
	divide_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_invoice = 100 }
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = IRQ
			has_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_tracked
			NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_investment }
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_investment_quote > 0 }
			check_variable = { var = treasury value = COLDWAR_USA_KS_invoice compare = greater_than_or_equals }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = COLDWAR_USA_KS_invoice }
		multiply_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -1 }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_investment
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_security
		remove_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_security_programme
		remove_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_investment_programme
		add_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_investment_programme
		update_money_dirty_variable = yes
	}
}

	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = USA
			country_exists = PER
		}
		PER = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_tracked }
				}
				set_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_tracked
				set_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_unrest = 40 }
				set_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_stabilisation = 0 }
				set_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_organisation = 0 }
				set_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_qualifying_days = 0 }
				set_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_warning_days = 0 }
				COLDWAR_USA_KS_refresh_host = yes
			}
		}
	}
}

				NOT = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_settlement_protects_per = yes }

		if = {

			}

		}
		if = {

				num_divisions_in_states = { count > 1 states = { 408 } }

				COLDWAR_USA_KS_forces_per = yes
			}

				COLDWAR_USA_KS_forces_per = yes

		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_legacy_effect = COLDWAR_USA_KS_stabilisation }
		multiply_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_legacy_effect = -0.08 }

			}

			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_investment_weeks > 5 }
				}
				add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_drift = -2.1 }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				COLDWAR_USA_KS_rights_per = yes
			}

			add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_stabilisation = 1.4 }
		}
		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_unrest = COLDWAR_USA_KS_drift }
		clamp_variable = { var = COLDWAR_USA_KS_unrest min = 0 max = 100 }
		clamp_variable = { var = COLDWAR_USA_KS_stabilisation min = 0 max = 100 }
		COLDWAR_USA_KS_peace_tick_per = yes
		COLDWAR_USA_KS_repression_tick = yes

			limit = {
				COLDWAR_USA_KS_region_per = yes
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_unrest > 79 }
				NOT = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_settlement_protects_per = yes }
				COLDWAR_USA_KS_material_per = yes
				COLDWAR_USA_KS_funding_per = yes
				USA = { check_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_stage_per > 2 } }
				NOT = { has_war_with = KPI }
			}
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_below_days = 0 }
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_qualifying_days > 29 }
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_warning }
					}
					set_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_warning
					country_event = { id = COLDWAR_USA_KS.111 }
				}
				add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_warning_days = 7 }
				if = {
					limit = {
						check_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_warning_days > 44 }
					}
					set_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_mobilisation_ready
				}
			}
			else = {
				add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_qualifying_days = 7 }
			}
		}
		else = {
			add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_below_days = 7 }
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_below_days > 14 }
				}
				set_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_qualifying_days = 0 }
				set_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_warning_days = 0 }
				clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_warning
				clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_mobilisation_ready
			}
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = PER
			has_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_tracked
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_network_cooldown > 0 }
		}
		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_network_cooldown = -1 }
	}
}

COLDWAR_USA_KS_per_security = {
	set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_invoice = gdp_total }
	multiply_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_invoice = 2 }
	divide_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_invoice = 100 }
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = PER
			has_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_tracked
			NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_security }
			COLDWAR_USA_KS_forces_per = yes
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_security_quote > 0 }
			check_variable = { var = treasury value = COLDWAR_USA_KS_invoice compare = greater_than_or_equals }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = COLDWAR_USA_KS_invoice }
		multiply_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -1 }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_security
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_investment
		remove_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_security_programme
		remove_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_investment_programme
		add_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_security_programme
		update_money_dirty_variable = yes
	}
}

COLDWAR_USA_KS_per_investment = {
	set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_invoice = gdp_total }
	multiply_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_invoice = 1 }
	divide_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_invoice = 100 }
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = PER
			has_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_tracked
			NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_investment }
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_investment_quote > 0 }
			check_variable = { var = treasury value = COLDWAR_USA_KS_invoice compare = greater_than_or_equals }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = COLDWAR_USA_KS_invoice }
		multiply_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -1 }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_investment
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_security
		remove_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_security_programme
		remove_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_investment_programme
		add_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_investment_programme
		update_money_dirty_variable = yes
	}
}

COLDWAR_USA_KS_init_tur = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = USA
			country_exists = TUR
		}
		TUR = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_tracked }
				}
				set_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_tracked
				set_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_unrest = 40 }
				set_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_stabilisation = 0 }
				set_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_organisation = 0 }
				set_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_qualifying_days = 0 }
				set_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_warning_days = 0 }
				COLDWAR_USA_KS_refresh_host = yes
			}
		}
	}
}

COLDWAR_USA_KS_tick_tur = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = TUR
			IMP_is_1970_scenario = yes
			has_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_tracked
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_drift = 0 }
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_pacified }
			}
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_drift = 1 }
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_support = 0 }
		if = {
			limit = {
				USA = { check_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_stage_tur = 3 } }
				COLDWAR_USA_KS_funding_tur = yes
				NOT = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_settlement_protects_tur = yes }
			}
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_support = 5.32 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				USA = { check_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_stage_tur = 2 } }
				COLDWAR_USA_KS_funding_tur = yes
				NOT = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_settlement_protects_tur = yes }
			}
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_support = 2.85 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				USA = { check_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_stage_tur = 1 } }
				COLDWAR_USA_KS_funding_tur = yes
				NOT = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_settlement_protects_tur = yes }
			}
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_support = 1.33 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				COLDWAR_USA_KS_pressure_tur = yes
			}
			add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_drift = 9 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_trauma_weeks > 0 }
			}
			add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_drift = COLDWAR_USA_KS_trauma_drift }
		}
		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_drift = COLDWAR_USA_KS_support }
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_organisation_effect = COLDWAR_USA_KS_organisation }
		multiply_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_organisation_effect = 0.01 }
		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_drift = COLDWAR_USA_KS_organisation_effect }
		if = {
			limit = {
				num_divisions_in_states = { count > 0 states = { 162 } }
			}
			add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_drift = -1.2 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				num_divisions_in_states = { count > 1 states = { 162 } }
			}
			add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_drift = -1.2 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				num_divisions_in_states = { count > 2 states = { 162 } }
			}
			add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_drift = -1.2 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				num_divisions_in_states = { count > 3 states = { 162 } }
			}
			add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_drift = -1.2 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_security
				COLDWAR_USA_KS_forces_tur = yes
			}
			add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_drift = -3 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_security
				COLDWAR_USA_KS_forces_tur = yes
			}
			add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_stabilisation = 3.5 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_investment
			}
			add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_stabilisation = 0.98 }
		}
		else = {
			add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_stabilisation = -0.7 }
		}
		clamp_variable = { var = COLDWAR_USA_KS_stabilisation min = 0 max = 100 }
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_legacy_effect = COLDWAR_USA_KS_stabilisation }
		multiply_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_legacy_effect = -0.08 }
		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_drift = COLDWAR_USA_KS_legacy_effect }
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_investment
			}
			add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_investment_weeks = 1 }
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_investment_weeks > 5 }
				}
				add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_drift = -2.1 }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				COLDWAR_USA_KS_rights_tur = yes
			}
			add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_drift = -4.5 }
			add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_stabilisation = 1.4 }
		}
		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_unrest = COLDWAR_USA_KS_drift }
		clamp_variable = { var = COLDWAR_USA_KS_unrest min = 0 max = 100 }
		clamp_variable = { var = COLDWAR_USA_KS_stabilisation min = 0 max = 100 }
		COLDWAR_USA_KS_peace_tick_tur = yes
		COLDWAR_USA_KS_repression_tick = yes
		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_ui_dirty = 1 }
		if = {
			limit = {
				COLDWAR_USA_KS_region_tur = yes
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_unrest > 79 }
				NOT = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_settlement_protects_tur = yes }
				COLDWAR_USA_KS_material_tur = yes
				COLDWAR_USA_KS_funding_tur = yes
				USA = { check_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_stage_tur > 2 } }
				NOT = { has_war_with = KPT }
			}
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_below_days = 0 }
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_qualifying_days > 29 }
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_warning }
					}
					set_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_warning
					country_event = { id = COLDWAR_USA_KS.112 }
				}
				add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_warning_days = 7 }
				if = {
					limit = {
						check_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_warning_days > 44 }
					}
					set_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_mobilisation_ready
				}
			}
			else = {
				add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_qualifying_days = 7 }
			}
		}
		else = {
			add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_below_days = 7 }
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_below_days > 14 }
				}
				set_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_qualifying_days = 0 }
				set_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_warning_days = 0 }
				clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_warning
				clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_mobilisation_ready
			}
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = TUR
			has_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_tracked
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_network_cooldown > 0 }
		}
		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_network_cooldown = -1 }
	}
}

		limit = {
			tag = SYR
			has_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_tracked
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_network_cooldown > 0 }
		}
		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_network_cooldown = -1 }
	}
}

COLDWAR_USA_KS_syr_security = {
	set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_invoice = gdp_total }
	multiply_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_invoice = 2 }
	divide_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_invoice = 100 }
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = SYR
			has_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_tracked
			NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_security }
			COLDWAR_USA_KS_forces_syr = yes
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_security_quote > 0 }
			check_variable = { var = treasury value = COLDWAR_USA_KS_invoice compare = greater_than_or_equals }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = COLDWAR_USA_KS_invoice }
		multiply_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -1 }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_security
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_investment
		remove_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_security_programme
		remove_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_investment_programme
		add_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_security_programme
		update_money_dirty_variable = yes
	}
}

COLDWAR_USA_KS_syr_investment = {
	set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_invoice = gdp_total }
	multiply_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_invoice = 1 }
	divide_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_invoice = 100 }
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = SYR
			has_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_tracked
			NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_investment }
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_investment_quote > 0 }
			check_variable = { var = treasury value = COLDWAR_USA_KS_invoice compare = greater_than_or_equals }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = COLDWAR_USA_KS_invoice }
		multiply_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -1 }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_investment
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_security
		remove_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_security_programme
		remove_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_investment_programme
		add_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_investment_programme
		update_money_dirty_variable = yes
	}
}

COLDWAR_USA_KS_count_campaigns = {
	set_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_campaign_count = 0 }
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_stage_irq > 0 }
		}
		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_campaign_count = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_stage_per > 0 }
		}
		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_campaign_count = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_stage_tur > 0 }
		}
		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_campaign_count = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_stage_syr > 0 }
		}
		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_campaign_count = 1 }
	}
}

			}

COLDWAR_USA_KS_uprising_irq = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = IRQ
			COLDWAR_USA_KS_mobilisation_irq = yes
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_qualifying_days > 29 }
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_warning_days > 44 }
			OR = { NOT = { country_exists = KUR } KUR = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_participate_irq } }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_held_states = 0 }
		164 = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					is_owned_and_controlled_by = IRQ is_fully_controlled_by = IRQ
				}
				IRQ = {
					add_to_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_held_states = 1 }
				}
			}
		}
		633 = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					is_owned_and_controlled_by = IRQ is_fully_controlled_by = IRQ
				}
				IRQ = {
					add_to_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_held_states = 1 }
				}
			}
		}
		630 = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					is_owned_and_controlled_by = IRQ is_fully_controlled_by = IRQ
				}
				IRQ = {
					add_to_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_held_states = 1 }
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_held_states > 1 }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { country_exists = KUR }
				}
				set_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_new_kur
			}
			164 = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						is_owned_and_controlled_by = IRQ is_fully_controlled_by = IRQ
					}
					add_core_of = KUR
					KUR = { transfer_state = 164 }
				}
			}
			633 = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						is_owned_and_controlled_by = IRQ is_fully_controlled_by = IRQ
					}
					add_core_of = KUR
					KUR = { transfer_state = 633 }
				}
			}
			630 = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						is_owned_and_controlled_by = IRQ is_fully_controlled_by = IRQ
					}
					add_core_of = KUR
					KUR = { transfer_state = 630 }
				}
			}
			KUR = {
				set_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_regional_actor
				set_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_cache_offer_irq
				if = {
					limit = {
						IRQ = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_new_kur }
					}
					COLDWAR_USA_KS_initialize_new_actor = yes
					set_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_recognition_requested
					if = {
						limit = {
							164 = { is_owned_and_controlled_by = KUR }
						}
						set_capital = { state = 164 remember_old_capital = no }
						division_template = { name = "Regional Defence Column" is_locked = yes regiments = { Militia_Bat = { x = 0 y = 0 } Militia_Bat = { x = 0 y = 1 } } }
						164 = { create_unit = { division = "division_template = \"Regional Defence Column\" start_experience_factor = 0.1 start_equipment_factor = 0 start_manpower_factor = 0" owner = KUR } }
						KUR = {
							set_equipment_fraction = 0
						}
					}
					else_if = {
						limit = {
							633 = { is_owned_and_controlled_by = KUR }
						}
						set_capital = { state = 633 remember_old_capital = no }
						division_template = { name = "Regional Defence Column" is_locked = yes regiments = { Militia_Bat = { x = 0 y = 0 } Militia_Bat = { x = 0 y = 1 } } }
						633 = { create_unit = { division = "division_template = \"Regional Defence Column\" start_experience_factor = 0.1 start_equipment_factor = 0 start_manpower_factor = 0" owner = KUR } }
						KUR = {
							set_equipment_fraction = 0
						}
					}
					else_if = {
						limit = {
							630 = { is_owned_and_controlled_by = KUR }
						}
						set_capital = { state = 630 remember_old_capital = no }
						division_template = { name = "Regional Defence Column" is_locked = yes regiments = { Militia_Bat = { x = 0 y = 0 } Militia_Bat = { x = 0 y = 1 } } }
						630 = { create_unit = { division = "division_template = \"Regional Defence Column\" start_experience_factor = 0.1 start_equipment_factor = 0 start_manpower_factor = 0" owner = KUR } }
						KUR = {
							set_equipment_fraction = 0
						}
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						NOT = { has_war_with = IRQ }
					}
					declare_war_on = { target = IRQ type = annex_everything }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					COLDWAR_USA_gulf_puk_formed = yes
					1078 = { is_owned_and_controlled_by = IRQ is_fully_controlled_by = IRQ }
					OR = { NOT = { country_exists = PUK } PUK = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_participate_irq } }
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						NOT = { country_exists = PUK }
					}
					set_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_new_puk
				}
				1078 = { add_core_of = PUK }
				PUK = { transfer_state = 1078
				if = {
					limit = {
						IRQ = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_new_puk }
					}
					COLDWAR_USA_KS_initialize_new_actor = yes
					set_capital = { state = 1078 remember_old_capital = no }
					division_template = { name = "Regional Defence Column" is_locked = yes regiments = { Militia_Bat = { x = 0 y = 0 } Militia_Bat = { x = 0 y = 1 } } }
					1078 = { create_unit = { division = "division_template = \"Regional Defence Column\" start_experience_factor = 0.1 start_equipment_factor = 0 start_manpower_factor = 0" owner = PUK } }
					PUK = {
						set_equipment_fraction = 0
					}
				}
				set_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_regional_actor
				set_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_cache_offer_irq
				if = {
					limit = {
						NOT = { has_war_with = IRQ }
					}
					declare_war_on = { target = IRQ type = annex_everything }
				} }
			}
			set_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_uprising_occurred
			set_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_gulf_uprising_fired
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_qualifying_days = 0 }
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_warning_days = 0 }
			clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_warning
			clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_new_kur
			clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_new_puk
		}
	}
}

COLDWAR_USA_KS_uprising_per = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = PER
			COLDWAR_USA_KS_mobilisation_per = yes
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_qualifying_days > 29 }
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_warning_days > 44 }
			NOT = { country_exists = KPI }
		}
		408 = { add_core_of = KPI }
		KPI = { transfer_state = 408 }
		KPI = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_initialize_new_actor = yes
		set_capital = { state = 408 remember_old_capital = no }
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_recognition_requested
		division_template = { name = "Regional Defence Column" is_locked = yes regiments = { Militia_Bat = { x = 0 y = 0 } Militia_Bat = { x = 0 y = 1 } } }
		408 = { create_unit = { division = "division_template = \"Regional Defence Column\" start_experience_factor = 0.1 start_equipment_factor = 0 start_manpower_factor = 0" owner = KPI } }
		KPI = {
			set_equipment_fraction = 0
		}
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_cache_offer_per
		declare_war_on = { target = PER type = annex_everything } }
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_uprising_occurred
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_qualifying_days = 0 }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_warning_days = 0 }
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_warning
	}
}

COLDWAR_USA_KS_refresh_host = {
	COLDWAR_USA_KS_refresh_fiscal_host = yes
	set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_old_unrest = COLDWAR_USA_KS_ui_unrest }
	set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_old_legacy = COLDWAR_USA_KS_ui_legacy }
	set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_old_warning = COLDWAR_USA_KS_ui_warning }
	set_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_ui_unrest = COLDWAR_USA_KS_unrest }
	round_variable = COLDWAR_USA_KS_ui_unrest
	set_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_ui_legacy = 0 }
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_stabilisation > 50 }
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_ui_legacy = 2 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_stabilisation > 5 }
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_ui_legacy = 1 }
	}
	set_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_ui_warning = 0 }
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_warning
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_ui_warning = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = { NOT = { check_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_ui_unrest = COLDWAR_USA_KS_old_unrest } } NOT = { check_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_ui_legacy = COLDWAR_USA_KS_old_legacy } } NOT = { check_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_ui_warning = COLDWAR_USA_KS_old_warning } } }
		}
		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_ui_dirty = 1 }
	}
}

COLDWAR_USA_KS_migrate_irq = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			IMP_is_1970_scenario = yes
			country_exists = IRQ
			OR = { IRQ = { COLDWAR_IRQ_campaign_active = yes } IRQ = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_gulf_northern_campaign_tracked } USA = { check_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_gulf_cache > 0 } } }
		}
		USA = {
			COLDWAR_USA_KS_init_irq = yes
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_legacy_cache_migrated }
				}
				set_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_legacy_cache_migrated
				add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_cache_irq = COLDWAR_USA_gulf_cache }
				if = {
					limit = {
						check_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_gulf_support_days > 0 }
					}
					set_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_stage_irq = 2 }
					set_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_max_stage_irq = 2 }
					set_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_legacy_irq_rate = 1 }
				}
				set_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_gulf_cache = 0 }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_legacy_liaison_migrated }
				}
				set_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_legacy_liaison_migrated
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_gulf_support_kur
					}
					set_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_stage_irq = 2 }
					set_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_max_stage_irq = 2 }
					set_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_legacy_irq_rate = 1 }
					clear_array = COLDWAR_USA_KS_payees_irq
					add_to_array = { array = COLDWAR_USA_KS_payees_irq value = KUR }
					KUR = {
						set_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_partner_irq
						if = {
							limit = {
								has_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_gulf_organized_opposition
							}
							set_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_participate_irq
						}
					}
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_gulf_support_puk
					}
					set_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_stage_irq = 2 }
					set_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_max_stage_irq = 2 }
					set_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_legacy_irq_rate = 1 }
					clear_array = COLDWAR_USA_KS_payees_irq
					add_to_array = { array = COLDWAR_USA_KS_payees_irq value = PUK }
					PUK = {
						set_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_partner_irq
						if = {
							limit = {
								has_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_gulf_organized_opposition
							}
							set_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_participate_irq
						}
					}
				}
				clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_gulf_support_kur
				clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_gulf_support_puk
				KUR = { remove_ideas = COLDWAR_KUR_us_advisers }
				PUK = { remove_ideas = COLDWAR_KUR_us_advisers }
				if = {
					limit = {
						KUR = { OR = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_gulf_cache_received has_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_gulf_initial_aid_received } }
					}
					set_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_delivered_irq_kur = 1000 }
					KUR = { set_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_partner_irq }
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						PUK = { OR = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_gulf_cache_received has_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_gulf_initial_aid_received } }
					}
					set_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_delivered_irq_puk = 1000 }
					PUK = { set_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_partner_irq }
				}
			}
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_gulf_support_days = 0 }
			COLDWAR_USA_KS_count_campaigns = yes
		}
		IRQ = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_legacy_unrest_migrated }
				}
				set_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_legacy_unrest_migrated
				if = {
					limit = {
						OR = { 164 = { has_resistance = yes resistance > 79 } 633 = { has_resistance = yes resistance > 79 } 630 = { has_resistance = yes resistance > 79 } 1078 = { has_resistance = yes resistance > 79 } }
					}
					set_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_unrest = 80 }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_legacy_security_migrated }
				}
				set_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_legacy_security_migrated
				if = {
					limit = {
						OR = { has_idea = COLDWAR_USA_gulf_security_small has_idea = COLDWAR_USA_gulf_security_major }
					}
					set_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_security
					add_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_security_programme
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						OR = { 164 = { check_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_gulf_security_local_days > 29 } } 633 = { check_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_gulf_security_local_days > 29 } } 630 = { check_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_gulf_security_local_days > 29 } } 1078 = { check_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_gulf_security_local_days > 29 } } }
					}
					set_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_stabilisation = 15 }
				}
				remove_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_gulf_security_small
				remove_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_gulf_security_major
				clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_gulf_security_delivery
				164 = { remove_resistance_target = 19709102 }
				633 = { remove_resistance_target = 19709102 }
				630 = { remove_resistance_target = 19709102 }
				1078 = { remove_resistance_target = 19709102 }
			}
			164 = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						OR = { has_state_flag = COLDWAR_USA_gulf_seeded has_state_flag = COLDWAR_USA_gulf_owns_forced_override }
					}
					clr_state_flag = COLDWAR_USA_gulf_seeded
					remove_resistance_target = 19709101
					remove_resistance_target = 19709102
					if = {
						limit = {
							has_state_flag = COLDWAR_USA_gulf_owns_forced_override
							NOT = { has_state_flag = MD_protests_active@IRQ }
						}
						force_enable_resistance = { clear = yes occupier = IRQ }
						if = {
							limit = {
								is_owned_and_controlled_by = IRQ
							}
							cancel_resistance = yes
						}
						clr_state_flag = COLDWAR_USA_gulf_owns_forced_override
					}
				}
			}
			633 = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						OR = { has_state_flag = COLDWAR_USA_gulf_seeded has_state_flag = COLDWAR_USA_gulf_owns_forced_override }
					}
					clr_state_flag = COLDWAR_USA_gulf_seeded
					remove_resistance_target = 19709101
					remove_resistance_target = 19709102
					if = {
						limit = {
							has_state_flag = COLDWAR_USA_gulf_owns_forced_override
							NOT = { has_state_flag = MD_protests_active@IRQ }
						}
						force_enable_resistance = { clear = yes occupier = IRQ }
						if = {
							limit = {
								is_owned_and_controlled_by = IRQ
							}
							cancel_resistance = yes
						}
						clr_state_flag = COLDWAR_USA_gulf_owns_forced_override
					}
				}
			}
			630 = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						OR = { has_state_flag = COLDWAR_USA_gulf_seeded has_state_flag = COLDWAR_USA_gulf_owns_forced_override }
					}
					clr_state_flag = COLDWAR_USA_gulf_seeded
					remove_resistance_target = 19709101
					remove_resistance_target = 19709102
					if = {
						limit = {
							has_state_flag = COLDWAR_USA_gulf_owns_forced_override
							NOT = { has_state_flag = MD_protests_active@IRQ }
						}
						force_enable_resistance = { clear = yes occupier = IRQ }
						if = {
							limit = {
								is_owned_and_controlled_by = IRQ
							}
							cancel_resistance = yes
						}
						clr_state_flag = COLDWAR_USA_gulf_owns_forced_override
					}
				}
			}
			1078 = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						OR = { has_state_flag = COLDWAR_USA_gulf_seeded has_state_flag = COLDWAR_USA_gulf_owns_forced_override }
					}
					clr_state_flag = COLDWAR_USA_gulf_seeded
					remove_resistance_target = 19709101
					remove_resistance_target = 19709102
					if = {
						limit = {
							has_state_flag = COLDWAR_USA_gulf_owns_forced_override
							NOT = { has_state_flag = MD_protests_active@IRQ }
						}
						force_enable_resistance = { clear = yes occupier = IRQ }
						if = {
							limit = {
								is_owned_and_controlled_by = IRQ
							}
							cancel_resistance = yes
						}
						clr_state_flag = COLDWAR_USA_gulf_owns_forced_override
					}
				}
			}
			COLDWAR_USA_KS_refresh_host = yes
		}
	}
}

COLDWAR_USA_KS_legacy_irq_start = {
	COLDWAR_USA_KS_count_campaigns = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_USA_KS_legacy_irq_ready = yes
			NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_legacy_gulf_paid }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_invoice = gdp_total }
		multiply_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_invoice = 0.025 }
		divide_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_invoice = 100 }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_invoice > 0 }
				check_variable = { var = treasury value = COLDWAR_USA_KS_invoice compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = COLDWAR_USA_KS_invoice }
			multiply_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -1 }
			modify_treasury_effect = yes
			COLDWAR_USA_KS_init_irq = yes
			add_political_power = -25
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_weapons_1 amount = -1000 }
			add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_cache_irq = 1000 }
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_legacy_irq_cash = COLDWAR_USA_KS_invoice }
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_legacy_irq_rate = 1 }
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_stage_irq = 3 }
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_max_stage_irq = 3 }
			set_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_contact_irq
			set_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_legacy_gulf_paid
			clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_legacy_gulf_pending
			set_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_gulf_initial_package_used
			COLDWAR_USA_KS_count_campaigns = yes
			COLDWAR_USA_KS_reconcile_aid = yes
		}
	}
}

COLDWAR_USA_KS_legacy_irq_preview = {
	effect_tooltip = {
		add_political_power = -25
		# IMPERIA COLD WAR — one treasury line like MD (24.11.2026)
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = gdp_total }
		multiply_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -0.025 }
		divide_temp_variable = { treasury_change = 100 }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_weapons_1 amount = -1000 }
		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_preview_material = 1000 tooltip = COLDWAR_USA_KS_material_tt }
		# IMPERIA COLD WAR — one weekly budget line like MD (24.11.2026)
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_preview_weekly_amount = gdp_total }
		multiply_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_preview_weekly_amount = -0.005 }
		divide_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_preview_weekly_amount = 100 }
		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_preview_weekly = COLDWAR_USA_KS_preview_weekly_amount tooltip = COLDWAR_USA_KS_weekly_tt }
	}

		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_preview_defense_bonus_against = 0.08 tooltip = COLDWAR_USA_gulf_preview_mod_defense_bonus_against_tt }
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = USA.gdp_total }
		multiply_temp_variable = { treasury_change = 0.025 }
		divide_temp_variable = { treasury_change = 100 }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes }
	}
	effect_tooltip = {
		USA = { set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_preview_material = COLDWAR_USA_KS_cache_irq } add_to_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_preview_material = 1000 } }
		custom_effect_tooltip = COLDWAR_USA_KS_if_accept_puk_tt
		USA = { add_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_aid_irq_puk_legacy_usa
		 }
		PUK = { add_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_aid_irq_puk_legacy_partner
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_weapons_1 amount = USA.COLDWAR_USA_KS_preview_material producer = USA }

		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_preview_defense_bonus_against = 0.08 tooltip = COLDWAR_USA_gulf_preview_mod_defense_bonus_against_tt }
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = USA.gdp_total }
		multiply_temp_variable = { treasury_change = 0.025 }
		divide_temp_variable = { treasury_change = 100 }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes }
	}
	unlock_decision_tooltip = COLDWAR_USA_KS_irq_cache_kur
	unlock_decision_tooltip = COLDWAR_USA_KS_irq_cache_puk
}

COLDWAR_USA_KS_local_rights_end_irq = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = { tag = KUR tag = PUK }
			OR = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_rights_irq_pending has_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_rights_irq_consent }
		}
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_rights_irq_pending
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_rights_irq_consent
		IRQ = { clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_domestic_rights_signed clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_rights_irq_accepted remove_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_domestic_rights }
		USA = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_deal_irq_settlement_end = yes COLDWAR_USA_KS_reconcile_deals = yes }
	}
}

COLDWAR_USA_KS_local_rights_preview_per = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			USA = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_deal_irn_rights_signed }
		}
		effect_tooltip = {
			custom_effect_tooltip = COLDWAR_USA_KS_if_accept_tt

			add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_preview_police_cost_multiplier_modifier = -0.05 tooltip = COLDWAR_USA_KS_mod_police_cost_multiplier_modifier_tt }

	else = {

COLDWAR_USA_KS_misc_reconcile = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = USA
			IMP_is_1970_scenario = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_chile_funding
				country_exists = CHL
				CHL = { NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_finance_oversight } }
			}
			CHL = { add_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_chile_pressure }
		}
		else = {
			CHL = { remove_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_chile_pressure }
		}
	}
}

COLDWAR_USA_KS_reconcile_domestic = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = IRQ
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				COLDWAR_USA_KS_domestic_rights_irq = yes
				NOT = { USA = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_deal_irq_settlement_active = yes } }
			}
			add_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_domestic_rights
		}
		else = {
			remove_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_domestic_rights
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = PER
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				COLDWAR_USA_KS_domestic_rights_per = yes
				NOT = { USA = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_deal_irn_rights_active = yes } }
			}
			add_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_domestic_rights
		}
		else = {
			remove_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_domestic_rights
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = TUR
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				COLDWAR_USA_KS_domestic_rights_tur = yes
				NOT = { USA = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_deal_tur_rights_active = yes } }
			}
			add_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_domestic_rights
		}
		else = {
			remove_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_domestic_rights
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = SYR
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				COLDWAR_USA_KS_domestic_rights_syr = yes
				NOT = { USA = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_deal_syr_rights_active = yes } }
			}
			add_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_domestic_rights
		}
		else = {
			remove_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_domestic_rights
		}
	}
}

}

COLDWAR_USA_KS_reconciliation_total = {
	set_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_reconciliation_weeks = COLDWAR_USA_KS_qualifying_weeks }
	add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_reconciliation_weeks = COLDWAR_USA_KS_reform_credit }
	clamp_variable = { var = COLDWAR_USA_KS_reconciliation_weeks min = 0 max = 520 }
}

COLDWAR_USA_KS_peace_tick_irq = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_pacified
			NOT = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_rights_irq = yes
			164 = { is_owned_and_controlled_by = IRQ is_fully_controlled_by = IRQ }
			633 = { is_owned_and_controlled_by = IRQ is_fully_controlled_by = IRQ }
			630 = { is_owned_and_controlled_by = IRQ is_fully_controlled_by = IRQ }
			1078 = { is_owned_and_controlled_by = IRQ is_fully_controlled_by = IRQ }
			NOT = { has_war_with = USA }
			NOT = { has_war_with = KUR }
			NOT = { has_war_with = PUK } }
		}
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_pacified
		remove_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_consolidated_compact
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_qualifying_weeks = 156 }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_breakdown_weeks = 0 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_pacified }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				COLDWAR_USA_KS_reconciliation_conditions_irq = yes
				NOT = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_pressure_irq = yes }
			}
			add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_qualifying_weeks = 1 }
		}
		else = {
			add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_qualifying_weeks = -2 }
		}
	}
	clamp_variable = { var = COLDWAR_USA_KS_qualifying_weeks min = 0 max = 520 }
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_pacified
			COLDWAR_USA_KS_pressure_irq = yes
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_unrest > 19 }
		}
		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_breakdown_weeks = 1 }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_breakdown_weeks > 25 }
			}
			clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_pacified
			remove_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_consolidated_compact
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_qualifying_weeks = 156 }
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_breakdown_weeks = 0 }
		}
	}
	else = {
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_breakdown_weeks = 0 }
	}
	COLDWAR_USA_KS_reconciliation_total = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_pressure_weeks > 0 }
		}
		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_pressure_weeks = -1 }
	}
}

COLDWAR_USA_KS_irq_consolidate = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = IRQ
			has_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_tracked
			NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_pacified }
			COLDWAR_USA_KS_reconciliation_conditions_irq = yes
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = COLDWAR_USA_KS_pressure_clear_tt
				NOT = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_pressure_irq = yes }
			}
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = COLDWAR_USA_KS_reconciliation_tt
				check_variable = { var = COLDWAR_USA_KS_reconciliation_weeks value = 520 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
				check_variable = { var = COLDWAR_USA_KS_qualifying_weeks value = 312 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			}
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_settlement_quote > 0 }
			check_variable = { var = treasury value = COLDWAR_USA_KS_settlement_quote compare = greater_than_or_equals }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_invoice = gdp_total }
		multiply_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_invoice = 10 }
		divide_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_invoice = 100 }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_invoice > 0 }
				check_variable = { var = treasury value = COLDWAR_USA_KS_invoice compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = COLDWAR_USA_KS_invoice }
			multiply_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -1 }
			modify_treasury_effect = yes
			set_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_pacified
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_unrest = 0 }
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_organisation = 0 }
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_breakdown_weeks = 0 }
			add_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_consolidated_compact
			COLDWAR_USA_KS_refresh_host = yes
			update_money_dirty_variable = yes
		}
	}
}

			custom_trigger_tooltip = { tooltip = COLDWAR_USA_KS_reform_previous_4_tt has_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_reform_3 }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_invoice = gdp_total }
		multiply_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_invoice = 18 }
		divide_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_invoice = 100 }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_invoice > 0 }
				check_variable = { var = treasury value = COLDWAR_USA_KS_invoice compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = COLDWAR_USA_KS_invoice }
			multiply_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -1 }
			modify_treasury_effect = yes
			set_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_reform_4
			add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_reform_credit = 52 }
			clamp_variable = { var = COLDWAR_USA_KS_reform_credit min = 0 max = 208 }
			COLDWAR_USA_KS_reconciliation_total = yes
			COLDWAR_USA_KS_refresh_host = yes
		}
	}
}

COLDWAR_USA_KS_irq_intensify = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_USA_KS_usa_actor = yes
			has_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_contact_irq
			IRQ = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_tracked check_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_pressure_weeks = 0 } }
			COLDWAR_USA_KS_region_irq = yes
			COLDWAR_USA_KS_confrontation_irq = yes
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = COLDWAR_USA_KS_pressure_ready_tt
				COLDWAR_USA_KS_funding_irq = yes
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_stage_irq > 2 }
				NOT = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_deal_irq_settlement_active = yes }
			}
			COLDWAR_USA_KS_route_irq = yes
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = COLDWAR_USA_KS_treasury_gdp_tt
				check_variable = { gdp_total > 0 }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_us_intensify_quote > 0 }
				check_variable = { var = treasury value = COLDWAR_USA_KS_us_intensify_quote compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			}
			custom_trigger_tooltip = { tooltip = COLDWAR_USA_KS_equipment_tt has_equipment = { infantry_weapons_1 > 999 } }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_invoice = gdp_total }
		multiply_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_invoice = 0.0025 }
		divide_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_invoice = 100 }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_invoice > 0 }
				check_variable = { var = treasury value = COLDWAR_USA_KS_invoice compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = COLDWAR_USA_KS_invoice }
			multiply_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -1 }
			modify_treasury_effect = yes
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_weapons_1 amount = -1000 }
			add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_cache_irq = 1000 }
			IRQ = {
				set_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_pressure_weeks = 26 }
				add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_organisation = 10 }
				clamp_variable = { var = COLDWAR_USA_KS_organisation min = 0 max = 100 }
				COLDWAR_USA_KS_refresh_host = yes
			}
			COLDWAR_USA_KS_reconcile_aid = yes
			update_money_dirty_variable = yes
		}
	}
}

COLDWAR_USA_KS_peace_tick_per = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_pacified
			NOT = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_rights_per = yes
			408 = { is_owned_and_controlled_by = PER is_fully_controlled_by = PER }
			NOT = { has_war_with = USA }
			NOT = { has_war_with = KPI } }
		}
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_pacified
		remove_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_consolidated_compact
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_qualifying_weeks = 156 }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_breakdown_weeks = 0 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_pacified }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				COLDWAR_USA_KS_reconciliation_conditions_per = yes
				NOT = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_pressure_per = yes }
			}
			add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_qualifying_weeks = 1 }
		}
		else = {
			add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_qualifying_weeks = -2 }
		}
	}
	clamp_variable = { var = COLDWAR_USA_KS_qualifying_weeks min = 0 max = 520 }
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_pacified
			COLDWAR_USA_KS_pressure_per = yes
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_unrest > 19 }
		}
		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_breakdown_weeks = 1 }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_breakdown_weeks > 25 }
			}
			clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_pacified
			remove_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_consolidated_compact
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_qualifying_weeks = 156 }
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_breakdown_weeks = 0 }
		}
	}
	else = {
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_breakdown_weeks = 0 }
	}
	COLDWAR_USA_KS_reconciliation_total = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_pressure_weeks > 0 }
		}
		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_pressure_weeks = -1 }
	}
}

COLDWAR_USA_KS_per_consolidate = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = PER

				tooltip = COLDWAR_USA_KS_pressure_clear_tt
				NOT = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_pressure_per = yes }
			}
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = COLDWAR_USA_KS_reconciliation_tt
				check_variable = { var = COLDWAR_USA_KS_reconciliation_weeks value = 520 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
				check_variable = { var = COLDWAR_USA_KS_qualifying_weeks value = 312 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			}
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_settlement_quote > 0 }
			check_variable = { var = treasury value = COLDWAR_USA_KS_settlement_quote compare = greater_than_or_equals }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_invoice = gdp_total }
		multiply_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_invoice = 10 }
		divide_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_invoice = 100 }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_invoice > 0 }
				check_variable = { var = treasury value = COLDWAR_USA_KS_invoice compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = COLDWAR_USA_KS_invoice }
			multiply_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -1 }
			modify_treasury_effect = yes
			set_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_pacified
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_unrest = 0 }
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_organisation = 0 }
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_breakdown_weeks = 0 }
			add_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_consolidated_compact

COLDWAR_USA_KS_tur_intensify = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_USA_KS_usa_actor = yes
			has_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_contact_tur
			TUR = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_tracked check_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_pressure_weeks = 0 } }
			COLDWAR_USA_KS_region_tur = yes
			COLDWAR_USA_KS_confrontation_tur = yes
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = COLDWAR_USA_KS_pressure_ready_tt
				COLDWAR_USA_KS_funding_tur = yes
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_stage_tur > 2 }
				NOT = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_deal_tur_rights_active = yes }
			}
			COLDWAR_USA_KS_route_tur = yes
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = COLDWAR_USA_KS_treasury_gdp_tt
				check_variable = { gdp_total > 0 }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_us_intensify_quote > 0 }
				check_variable = { var = treasury value = COLDWAR_USA_KS_us_intensify_quote compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			}
			custom_trigger_tooltip = { tooltip = COLDWAR_USA_KS_equipment_tt has_equipment = { infantry_weapons_1 > 999 } }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_invoice = gdp_total }
		multiply_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_invoice = 0.0025 }
		divide_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_invoice = 100 }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_invoice > 0 }
				check_variable = { var = treasury value = COLDWAR_USA_KS_invoice compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = COLDWAR_USA_KS_invoice }
			multiply_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -1 }
			modify_treasury_effect = yes
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_weapons_1 amount = -1000 }
			add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_cache_tur = 1000 }
			TUR = {
				set_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_pressure_weeks = 26 }
				add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_organisation = 10 }
				clamp_variable = { var = COLDWAR_USA_KS_organisation min = 0 max = 100 }
				COLDWAR_USA_KS_refresh_host = yes
			}
			COLDWAR_USA_KS_reconcile_aid = yes
			update_money_dirty_variable = yes
		}
	}
}

COLDWAR_USA_KS_peace_tick_syr = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_pacified
			NOT = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_rights_syr = yes
			193 = { is_owned_and_controlled_by = SYR is_fully_controlled_by = SYR }
			NOT = { has_war_with = USA }
			NOT = { has_war_with = KPS } }
		}
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_pacified
		remove_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_consolidated_compact
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_qualifying_weeks = 156 }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_breakdown_weeks = 0 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_pacified }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				COLDWAR_USA_KS_reconciliation_conditions_syr = yes
				NOT = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_pressure_syr = yes }
			}
			add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_qualifying_weeks = 1 }
		}
		else = {
			add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_qualifying_weeks = -2 }
		}
	}
	clamp_variable = { var = COLDWAR_USA_KS_qualifying_weeks min = 0 max = 520 }
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_pacified
			COLDWAR_USA_KS_pressure_syr = yes
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_unrest > 19 }
		}
		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_breakdown_weeks = 1 }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_breakdown_weeks > 25 }
			}
			clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_pacified
			remove_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_consolidated_compact
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_qualifying_weeks = 156 }
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_breakdown_weeks = 0 }
		}
	}
	else = {
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_breakdown_weeks = 0 }
	}
	COLDWAR_USA_KS_reconciliation_total = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_pressure_weeks > 0 }
		}
		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_pressure_weeks = -1 }
	}
}

COLDWAR_USA_KS_syr_consolidate = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = SYR
			has_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_tracked
			NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_pacified }

				tooltip = COLDWAR_USA_KS_pressure_clear_tt
				NOT = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_pressure_syr = yes }
			}
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = COLDWAR_USA_KS_reconciliation_tt
				check_variable = { var = COLDWAR_USA_KS_reconciliation_weeks value = 520 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
				check_variable = { var = COLDWAR_USA_KS_qualifying_weeks value = 312 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			}
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_settlement_quote > 0 }
			check_variable = { var = treasury value = COLDWAR_USA_KS_settlement_quote compare = greater_than_or_equals }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_invoice = gdp_total }
		multiply_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_invoice = 10 }
		divide_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_invoice = 100 }

			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_invoice > 0 }
				check_variable = { var = treasury value = COLDWAR_USA_KS_invoice compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = COLDWAR_USA_KS_invoice }
			multiply_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -1 }
			modify_treasury_effect = yes
			set_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_pacified
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_unrest = 0 }
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_organisation = 0 }
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_breakdown_weeks = 0 }
			add_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_consolidated_compact
			COLDWAR_USA_KS_refresh_host = yes
			update_money_dirty_variable = yes
		}
	}

				check_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_us_intensify_quote > 0 }
				check_variable = { var = treasury value = COLDWAR_USA_KS_us_intensify_quote compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			}
			custom_trigger_tooltip = { tooltip = COLDWAR_USA_KS_equipment_tt has_equipment = { infantry_weapons_1 > 999 } }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_invoice = gdp_total }
		multiply_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_invoice = 0.0025 }
		divide_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_invoice = 100 }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_invoice > 0 }
				check_variable = { var = treasury value = COLDWAR_USA_KS_invoice compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = COLDWAR_USA_KS_invoice }
			multiply_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -1 }
			modify_treasury_effect = yes
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_weapons_1 amount = -1000 }
			add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_cache_syr = 1000 }
			SYR = {
				set_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_pressure_weeks = 26 }
				add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_organisation = 10 }
				clamp_variable = { var = COLDWAR_USA_KS_organisation min = 0 max = 100 }
				COLDWAR_USA_KS_refresh_host = yes
			}
			COLDWAR_USA_KS_reconcile_aid = yes
			update_money_dirty_variable = yes
		}
	}
}

COLDWAR_USA_KS_repression_tick = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_repression_cooldown > 0 }
		}
		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_repression_cooldown = -1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_trauma_weeks > 0 }
		}
		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_trauma_weeks = -1 }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_trauma_weeks = 0 }
			}
			remove_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_repression_legacy
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_trauma_drift = 0 }
		}
	}
}

COLDWAR_USA_KS_irq_emergency = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = IRQ
			has_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_tracked
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = COLDWAR_USA_KS_repression_requirements_tt
				num_divisions_in_states = { count > 3 states = { 164 633 630 1078 } }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_unrest > 39 }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_repression_cooldown = 0 }
				OR = { 164 = { is_owned_and_controlled_by = IRQ is_fully_controlled_by = IRQ state_population > 1000 } 633 = { is_owned_and_controlled_by = IRQ is_fully_controlled_by = IRQ state_population > 1000 } 630 = { is_owned_and_controlled_by = IRQ is_fully_controlled_by = IRQ state_population > 1000 } 1078 = { is_owned_and_controlled_by = IRQ is_fully_controlled_by = IRQ state_population > 1000 } }
			}
			check_variable = { var = treasury value = COLDWAR_USA_KS_emergency_quote compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_emergency_quote > 0 }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_invoice = gdp_total }
		multiply_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_invoice = 2 }
		divide_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_invoice = 100 }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_invoice > 0 }
				check_variable = { var = treasury value = COLDWAR_USA_KS_invoice compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = COLDWAR_USA_KS_invoice }
			multiply_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -1 }
			modify_treasury_effect = yes
			if = {
				limit = {
					164 = { is_owned_and_controlled_by = IRQ is_fully_controlled_by = IRQ state_population > 1000 }
				}
				164 = {
					IRQ = {
						set_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_incident_state = 164 }
					}
					add_manpower = -1000
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					633 = { is_owned_and_controlled_by = IRQ is_fully_controlled_by = IRQ state_population > 1000 }
				}
				633 = {
					IRQ = {
						set_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_incident_state = 633 }
					}
					add_manpower = -1000
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					630 = { is_owned_and_controlled_by = IRQ is_fully_controlled_by = IRQ state_population > 1000 }
				}
				630 = {
					IRQ = {
						set_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_incident_state = 630 }
					}
					add_manpower = -1000
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					1078 = { is_owned_and_controlled_by = IRQ is_fully_controlled_by = IRQ state_population > 1000 }
				}
				1078 = {
					IRQ = {
						set_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_incident_state = 1078 }
					}
					add_manpower = -1000
				}
			}
			add_stability = -0.04
			add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_unrest = -25 }
			add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_stabilisation = 10 }
			add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_organisation = -10 }
			clamp_variable = { var = COLDWAR_USA_KS_unrest min = 0 max = 100 }
			clamp_variable = { var = COLDWAR_USA_KS_stabilisation min = 0 max = 100 }
			clamp_variable = { var = COLDWAR_USA_KS_organisation min = 0 max = 100 }
			clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_domestic_rights_signed
			remove_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_domestic_rights
			COLDWAR_USA_KS_deal_irq_settlement_end = yes
			USA = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_reconcile_deals = yes COLDWAR_USA_KS_reconcile_aid = yes }
			clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_IRQ_autonomy_fulfilled
			add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_qualifying_weeks = -52 }
			clamp_variable = { var = COLDWAR_USA_KS_qualifying_weeks min = 0 max = 520 }
			clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_pacified
			remove_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_consolidated_compact
			COLDWAR_USA_KS_reconciliation_total = yes
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_last_civilian_losses = 1000 }
			add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_civilian_losses = 1000 }
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_trauma_weeks = 104 }
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_trauma_drift = 0.75 }
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_repression_cooldown = 104 }
			add_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_repression_legacy
			country_event = { id = COLDWAR_USA_KS.210 }
			USA = { if = { limit = { exists = yes } country_event = { id = COLDWAR_USA_KS.220 } } }
			KUR = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						exists = yes
					}
					country_event = { id = COLDWAR_USA_KS.230 }
				}
			}
			PUK = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						exists = yes
					}
					country_event = { id = COLDWAR_USA_KS.230 }
				}
			}
			every_country = {
				limit = {
					exists = yes
					NOT = { tag = IRQ } NOT = { tag = USA } NOT = { tag = KUR } NOT = { tag = PUK }
				}
				country_event = { id = COLDWAR_USA_KS.240 }
			}
			COLDWAR_USA_KS_refresh_host = yes
			update_money_dirty_variable = yes
		}
	}
}

COLDWAR_USA_KS_tur_repression = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = TUR
			has_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_tracked
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = COLDWAR_USA_KS_repression_requirements_tt
				num_divisions_in_states = { count > 3 states = { 162 } }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_unrest > 39 }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_repression_cooldown = 0 }
				OR = { 162 = { is_owned_and_controlled_by = TUR is_fully_controlled_by = TUR state_population > 10000 } }
			}
			check_variable = { var = treasury value = COLDWAR_USA_KS_repression_quote compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_repression_quote > 0 }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_invoice = gdp_total }
		multiply_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_invoice = 4 }
		divide_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_invoice = 100 }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_invoice > 0 }
				check_variable = { var = treasury value = COLDWAR_USA_KS_invoice compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = COLDWAR_USA_KS_invoice }
			multiply_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -1 }
			modify_treasury_effect = yes
			if = {
				limit = {
					162 = { is_owned_and_controlled_by = TUR is_fully_controlled_by = TUR state_population > 10000 }
				}
				162 = {
					TUR = {
						set_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_incident_state = 162 }
					}
					add_manpower = -10000
				}
			}
			add_stability = -0.12
			add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_unrest = -50 }
			add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_stabilisation = 20 }
			add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_organisation = -20 }
			clamp_variable = { var = COLDWAR_USA_KS_unrest min = 0 max = 100 }
			clamp_variable = { var = COLDWAR_USA_KS_stabilisation min = 0 max = 100 }
			clamp_variable = { var = COLDWAR_USA_KS_organisation min = 0 max = 100 }
			clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_domestic_rights_signed
			remove_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_domestic_rights
			COLDWAR_USA_KS_deal_tur_rights_end = yes
			USA = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_reconcile_deals = yes COLDWAR_USA_KS_reconcile_aid = yes }
			add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_qualifying_weeks = -104 }
			clamp_variable = { var = COLDWAR_USA_KS_qualifying_weeks min = 0 max = 520 }
			clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_pacified
			remove_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_consolidated_compact
			COLDWAR_USA_KS_reconciliation_total = yes
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_last_civilian_losses = 10000 }
			add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_civilian_losses = 10000 }
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_trauma_weeks = 156 }
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_trauma_drift = 1.5 }
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_repression_cooldown = 104 }
			add_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_repression_legacy
