COLDWAR_USA_KS_cache_preview_irq_kur = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			USA = { check_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_legacy_irq_rate > 0 } }
		}
		effect_tooltip = {
			custom_effect_tooltip = COLDWAR_USA_KS_if_accept_kur_tt
			USA = { add_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_aid_irq_kur_legacy_usa
			 }
			KUR = { add_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_aid_irq_kur_legacy_partner
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_weapons_1 amount = USA.COLDWAR_USA_KS_cache_irq producer = USA }

			add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_preview_defense_bonus_against = 0.08 tooltip = COLDWAR_USA_gulf_preview_mod_defense_bonus_against_tt } }
		}
	}
	else = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				USA = { OR = { check_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_stage_irq > 2 } AND = { check_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_stage_irq = 0 } check_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_max_stage_irq > 2 } } } }
			}
			effect_tooltip = {
				custom_effect_tooltip = COLDWAR_USA_KS_if_accept_kur_tt
				USA = { add_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_aid_irq_kur_3_usa
				 }
				KUR = { add_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_aid_irq_kur_3_partner
				add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_weapons_1 amount = USA.COLDWAR_USA_KS_cache_irq producer = USA }
				 }
			}
		}
		else = {
			effect_tooltip = {
				custom_effect_tooltip = COLDWAR_USA_KS_if_accept_kur_tt
				USA = { add_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_aid_irq_kur_2_usa
				 }
				KUR = { add_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_aid_irq_kur_2_partner
				add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_weapons_1 amount = USA.COLDWAR_USA_KS_cache_irq producer = USA }
				 }
			}
		}
	}
	effect_tooltip = {
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = USA.COLDWAR_USA_KS_legacy_irq_cash }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
	}
}

COLDWAR_USA_KS_reconcile_aid = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_USA_KS_usa_actor = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				country_exists = KUR
				COLDWAR_USA_KS_funding_irq = yes
				NOT = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_settlement_protects_irq = yes }
				is_in_array = { array = COLDWAR_USA_KS_payees_irq value = KUR }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_stage_irq = 2 }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_legacy_irq_rate = 0 }
			}
			if = { limit = { NOT = { has_idea = COLDWAR_USA_KS_aid_irq_kur_2_usa } } add_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_aid_irq_kur_2_usa }
			KUR = { if = { limit = { NOT = { has_idea = COLDWAR_USA_KS_aid_irq_kur_2_partner } } add_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_aid_irq_kur_2_partner } }
		}
		else = {
			remove_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_aid_irq_kur_2_usa
			KUR = { remove_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_aid_irq_kur_2_partner }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				country_exists = KUR
				COLDWAR_USA_KS_funding_irq = yes
				NOT = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_settlement_protects_irq = yes }
				is_in_array = { array = COLDWAR_USA_KS_payees_irq value = KUR }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_stage_irq = 3 }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_legacy_irq_rate = 0 }
			}
			if = { limit = { NOT = { has_idea = COLDWAR_USA_KS_aid_irq_kur_3_usa } } add_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_aid_irq_kur_3_usa }
			KUR = { if = { limit = { NOT = { has_idea = COLDWAR_USA_KS_aid_irq_kur_3_partner } } add_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_aid_irq_kur_3_partner } }
		}
		else = {
			remove_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_aid_irq_kur_3_usa
			KUR = { remove_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_aid_irq_kur_3_partner }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				country_exists = KUR
				COLDWAR_USA_KS_funding_irq = yes
				NOT = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_settlement_protects_irq = yes }
				is_in_array = { array = COLDWAR_USA_KS_payees_irq value = KUR }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_legacy_irq_rate > 0 }
			}
			if = { limit = { NOT = { has_idea = COLDWAR_USA_KS_aid_irq_kur_legacy_usa } } add_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_aid_irq_kur_legacy_usa }
			KUR = { if = { limit = { NOT = { has_idea = COLDWAR_USA_KS_aid_irq_kur_legacy_partner } } add_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_aid_irq_kur_legacy_partner } }
		}
		else = {
			remove_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_aid_irq_kur_legacy_usa
			KUR = { remove_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_aid_irq_kur_legacy_partner }
		}
		if = {

				country_exists = PUK
				COLDWAR_USA_KS_funding_irq = yes
				NOT = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_settlement_protects_irq = yes }

				check_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_stage_irq = 2 }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_legacy_irq_rate = 0 }
			}
			if = { limit = { NOT = { has_idea = COLDWAR_USA_KS_aid_irq_puk_2_usa } } add_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_aid_irq_puk_2_usa }
			PUK = { if = { limit = { NOT = { has_idea = COLDWAR_USA_KS_aid_irq_puk_2_partner } } add_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_aid_irq_puk_2_partner } }
		}
		else = {
			remove_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_aid_irq_puk_2_usa
			PUK = { remove_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_aid_irq_puk_2_partner }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				country_exists = PUK
				COLDWAR_USA_KS_funding_irq = yes
				NOT = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_settlement_protects_irq = yes }
				is_in_array = { array = COLDWAR_USA_KS_payees_irq value = PUK }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_stage_irq = 3 }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_legacy_irq_rate = 0 }
			}
			if = { limit = { NOT = { has_idea = COLDWAR_USA_KS_aid_irq_puk_3_usa } } add_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_aid_irq_puk_3_usa }
			PUK = { if = { limit = { NOT = { has_idea = COLDWAR_USA_KS_aid_irq_puk_3_partner } } add_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_aid_irq_puk_3_partner } }
		}
		else = {
			remove_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_aid_irq_puk_3_usa
			PUK = { remove_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_aid_irq_puk_3_partner }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				country_exists = PUK
				COLDWAR_USA_KS_funding_irq = yes
				NOT = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_settlement_protects_irq = yes }
				is_in_array = { array = COLDWAR_USA_KS_payees_irq value = PUK }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_legacy_irq_rate > 0 }
			}
			if = { limit = { NOT = { has_idea = COLDWAR_USA_KS_aid_irq_puk_legacy_usa } } add_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_aid_irq_puk_legacy_usa }
			PUK = { if = { limit = { NOT = { has_idea = COLDWAR_USA_KS_aid_irq_puk_legacy_partner } } add_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_aid_irq_puk_legacy_partner } }
		}
		else = {
			remove_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_aid_irq_puk_legacy_usa
			PUK = { remove_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_aid_irq_puk_legacy_partner }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				country_exists = KPI
				COLDWAR_USA_KS_funding_per = yes
				NOT = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_settlement_protects_per = yes }
				is_in_array = { array = COLDWAR_USA_KS_payees_per value = KPI }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_stage_per = 2 }
			}
			if = { limit = { NOT = { has_idea = COLDWAR_USA_KS_aid_per_kpi_2_usa } } add_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_aid_per_kpi_2_usa }
			KPI = { if = { limit = { NOT = { has_idea = COLDWAR_USA_KS_aid_per_kpi_2_partner } } add_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_aid_per_kpi_2_partner } }
		}
		else = {
			remove_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_aid_per_kpi_2_usa
			KPI = { remove_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_aid_per_kpi_2_partner }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				country_exists = KPI
				COLDWAR_USA_KS_funding_per = yes
				NOT = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_settlement_protects_per = yes }
				is_in_array = { array = COLDWAR_USA_KS_payees_per value = KPI }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_stage_per = 3 }

				country_exists = KPT
				COLDWAR_USA_KS_funding_tur = yes
				NOT = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_settlement_protects_tur = yes }
				is_in_array = { array = COLDWAR_USA_KS_payees_tur value = KPT }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_stage_tur = 3 }
			}
			if = { limit = { NOT = { has_idea = COLDWAR_USA_KS_aid_tur_kpt_3_usa } } add_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_aid_tur_kpt_3_usa }
			KPT = { if = { limit = { NOT = { has_idea = COLDWAR_USA_KS_aid_tur_kpt_3_partner } } add_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_aid_tur_kpt_3_partner } }
		}

		}

				NOT = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_settlement_protects_syr = yes }
				is_in_array = { array = COLDWAR_USA_KS_payees_syr value = KPS }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_stage_syr = 2 }
			}
			if = { limit = { NOT = { has_idea = COLDWAR_USA_KS_aid_syr_kps_2_usa } } add_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_aid_syr_kps_2_usa }
			KPS = { if = { limit = { NOT = { has_idea = COLDWAR_USA_KS_aid_syr_kps_2_partner } } add_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_aid_syr_kps_2_partner } }
		}
		else = {
			remove_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_aid_syr_kps_2_usa
			KPS = { remove_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_aid_syr_kps_2_partner }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				country_exists = KPS
				COLDWAR_USA_KS_funding_syr = yes
				NOT = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_settlement_protects_syr = yes }
				is_in_array = { array = COLDWAR_USA_KS_payees_syr value = KPS }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_stage_syr = 3 }
			}
			if = { limit = { NOT = { has_idea = COLDWAR_USA_KS_aid_syr_kps_3_usa } } add_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_aid_syr_kps_3_usa }
			KPS = { if = { limit = { NOT = { has_idea = COLDWAR_USA_KS_aid_syr_kps_3_partner } } add_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_aid_syr_kps_3_partner } }
		}
		else = {
			remove_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_aid_syr_kps_3_usa
			KPS = { remove_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_aid_syr_kps_3_partner }
		}
	}
	COLDWAR_USA_KS_reconcile_underground = yes
}

COLDWAR_USA_KS_irq_finance_networks = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_USA_KS_usa_actor = yes
			has_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_contact_irq
			COLDWAR_USA_KS_region_irq = yes
			COLDWAR_USA_KS_confrontation_irq = yes
			COLDWAR_USA_KS_funding_irq = yes
			check_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_stage_irq > 1 }
			NOT = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_settlement_protects_irq = yes }
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = COLDWAR_USA_KS_underground_ready_tt
				IRQ = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_tracked NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_pacified } check_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_network_cooldown = 0 } check_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_organisation < 100 } }
			}
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = COLDWAR_USA_KS_treasury_gdp_tt
				check_variable = { gdp_total > 0 }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_us_networks_quote > 0 }
				check_variable = { var = treasury value = COLDWAR_USA_KS_us_networks_quote compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_invoice = gdp_total }
		multiply_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_invoice = 0.0005 }
		divide_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_invoice = 100 }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_invoice > 0 }
				check_variable = { var = treasury value = COLDWAR_USA_KS_invoice compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = COLDWAR_USA_KS_invoice }
			multiply_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -1 }
			modify_treasury_effect = yes
			IRQ = {
				add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_organisation = 15 }
				add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_unrest = 5 }
				set_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_network_cooldown = 13 }
				clamp_variable = { var = COLDWAR_USA_KS_organisation min = 0 max = 100 }
				clamp_variable = { var = COLDWAR_USA_KS_unrest min = 0 max = 100 }
				COLDWAR_USA_KS_refresh_host = yes
			}
			COLDWAR_USA_KS_reconcile_aid = yes
			update_money_dirty_variable = yes
		}
	}
}

COLDWAR_USA_KS_reconcile_underground = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_USA_KS_usa_actor = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				COLDWAR_USA_KS_funding_irq = yes
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_stage_irq = 2 }
				NOT = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_settlement_protects_irq = yes }
				NOT = { OR = { AND = { country_exists = KUR is_in_array = { array = COLDWAR_USA_KS_payees_irq value = KUR } } AND = { country_exists = PUK is_in_array = { array = COLDWAR_USA_KS_payees_irq value = PUK } } } }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_legacy_irq_rate = 0 }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { has_idea = COLDWAR_USA_KS_underground_irq_2 }
				}
				add_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_underground_irq_2
			}
		}
		else = {
			remove_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_underground_irq_2
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				COLDWAR_USA_KS_funding_irq = yes
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_stage_irq = 3 }
				NOT = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_settlement_protects_irq = yes }
				NOT = { OR = { AND = { country_exists = KUR is_in_array = { array = COLDWAR_USA_KS_payees_irq value = KUR } } AND = { country_exists = PUK is_in_array = { array = COLDWAR_USA_KS_payees_irq value = PUK } } } }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_legacy_irq_rate = 0 }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { has_idea = COLDWAR_USA_KS_underground_irq_3 }
				}
				add_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_underground_irq_3
			}
		}
		else = {
			remove_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_underground_irq_3
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				COLDWAR_USA_KS_funding_irq = yes
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_stage_irq = 3 }
				NOT = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_settlement_protects_irq = yes }
				NOT = { OR = { AND = { country_exists = KUR is_in_array = { array = COLDWAR_USA_KS_payees_irq value = KUR } } AND = { country_exists = PUK is_in_array = { array = COLDWAR_USA_KS_payees_irq value = PUK } } } }
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_legacy_irq_rate > 0 }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { has_idea = COLDWAR_USA_KS_underground_irq_legacy }
				}
				add_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_underground_irq_legacy
			}
		}
		else = {
			remove_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_underground_irq_legacy
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				COLDWAR_USA_KS_funding_per = yes
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_stage_per = 2 }
				NOT = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_settlement_protects_per = yes }
				NOT = { OR = { AND = { country_exists = KPI is_in_array = { array = COLDWAR_USA_KS_payees_per value = KPI } } } }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { has_idea = COLDWAR_USA_KS_underground_per_2 }
				}
				add_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_underground_per_2
			}
		}
		else = {
			remove_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_underground_per_2
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				COLDWAR_USA_KS_funding_per = yes
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_stage_per = 3 }
				NOT = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_settlement_protects_per = yes }
				NOT = { OR = { AND = { country_exists = KPI is_in_array = { array = COLDWAR_USA_KS_payees_per value = KPI } } } }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { has_idea = COLDWAR_USA_KS_underground_per_3 }
				}
				add_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_underground_per_3
			}
		}
		else = {
			remove_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_underground_per_3
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				COLDWAR_USA_KS_funding_tur = yes
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_stage_tur = 2 }
				NOT = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_settlement_protects_tur = yes }
				NOT = { OR = { AND = { country_exists = KPT is_in_array = { array = COLDWAR_USA_KS_payees_tur value = KPT } } } }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { has_idea = COLDWAR_USA_KS_underground_tur_2 }
				}
				add_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_underground_tur_2
			}
		}
		else = {
			remove_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_underground_tur_2
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				COLDWAR_USA_KS_funding_tur = yes
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_stage_tur = 3 }
				NOT = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_settlement_protects_tur = yes }
				NOT = { OR = { AND = { country_exists = KPT is_in_array = { array = COLDWAR_USA_KS_payees_tur value = KPT } } } }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { has_idea = COLDWAR_USA_KS_underground_tur_3 }
				}
				add_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_underground_tur_3
			}
		}
		else = {
			remove_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_underground_tur_3
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				COLDWAR_USA_KS_funding_syr = yes
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_stage_syr = 2 }
				NOT = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_settlement_protects_syr = yes }
				NOT = { OR = { AND = { country_exists = KPS is_in_array = { array = COLDWAR_USA_KS_payees_syr value = KPS } } } }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { has_idea = COLDWAR_USA_KS_underground_syr_2 }
				}
				add_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_underground_syr_2
			}
		}
		else = {
			remove_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_underground_syr_2
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				COLDWAR_USA_KS_funding_syr = yes
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_stage_syr = 3 }
				NOT = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_settlement_protects_syr = yes }
				NOT = { OR = { AND = { country_exists = KPS is_in_array = { array = COLDWAR_USA_KS_payees_syr value = KPS } } } }
			}
			if = {

					NOT = { has_idea = COLDWAR_USA_KS_underground_syr_3 }
				}

			}
		}
		else = {

		limit = {
			COLDWAR_USA_KS_ready_doctrine = yes
			NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_done_doctrine }
		}
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_done_doctrine
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_pending_doctrine
		add_political_power = -75
		add_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_policy_doctrine
		COLDWAR_USA_KS_reconcile_aid = yes
		update_money_dirty_variable = yes
	}

COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_exchange_offer = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_exchange_can_offer = yes
		}
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_exchange_authorised
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_exchange_pending
		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_exchange_revision = 1 }
		COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_refresh_quotes = yes
		CHI = { set_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_exchange_pending clear_variable = COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_exchange_review_revision country_event = { id = COLDWAR_USA_TD.1 } }
	}
}

COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_exchange_review = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = CHI
			has_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_exchange_pending
			USA = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_exchange_pending }
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_exchange_review_revision = USA.COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_exchange_revision }
	}
}

COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_exchange_accept = {
	USA = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_refresh_quotes = yes }
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_exchange_accept_valid = yes
			USA = { check_variable = { gdp_total > 0 } check_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_exchange_setup_quote > 0 } check_variable = { var = treasury value = COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_exchange_setup_quote compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
		}
		USA = { set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = gdp_total }
		multiply_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -0.0003 }
		divide_temp_variable = { treasury_change = 100 }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes set_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_exchange_signed clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_exchange_pending }
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_exchange_signed
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_exchange_pending
		USA = { set_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_exchange_end = global.num_days } add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_exchange_end = 1460 } country_event = { id = COLDWAR_USA_TD.11 days = 1460 } }
		USA = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_reconcile = yes }
		USA = { update_money_dirty_variable = yes }
		update_money_dirty_variable = yes
	}
}

COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_exchange_end = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = { tag = USA tag = CHI }
		}
		USA = { clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_exchange_signed clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_exchange_pending set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_exchange_cooldown days = 60 value = 1 } remove_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_exchange_usa update_money_dirty_variable = yes }
		CHI = { clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_exchange_signed clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_exchange_pending clear_variable = COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_exchange_review_revision remove_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_exchange_china update_money_dirty_variable = yes }
	}
}

		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_preview_research_speed_factor = 0.03 tooltip = COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_mod_research_speed_factor_tt }
		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_preview_bureaucracy_cost_multiplier_modifier = 0.01 tooltip = COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_mod_bureaucracy_cost_multiplier_modifier_tt }
		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_preview_foreign_influence_defense_modifier = -0.03 tooltip = COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_mod_foreign_influence_defense_modifier_tt }
		# IMPERIA COLD WAR — one weekly budget line like MD (24.11.2026)
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_preview_weekly_amount = USA.gdp_total }
		multiply_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_preview_weekly_amount = 0.00005 }

		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_preview_weekly = COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_preview_weekly_amount tooltip = COLDWAR_USA_KS_weekly_tt } }

		limit = {
			COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_industry_accept_valid = yes
			USA = { check_variable = { gdp_total > 0 } check_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_industry_setup_quote > 0 } check_variable = { var = treasury value = COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_industry_setup_quote compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
		}
		USA = { set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = gdp_total }
		multiply_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -0.001 }
		divide_temp_variable = { treasury_change = 100 }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes set_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_industry_signed clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_industry_pending }
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_industry_signed
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_industry_pending
		USA = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_reconcile = yes }
		USA = { update_money_dirty_variable = yes }
		update_money_dirty_variable = yes
	}
}

COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_refresh_quotes = {
	set_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_exchange_setup_quote = gdp_total }
	multiply_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_exchange_setup_quote = 0.0003 }
	divide_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_exchange_setup_quote = 100 }
	set_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_industry_setup_quote = gdp_total }
	multiply_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_industry_setup_quote = 0.001 }
	divide_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_industry_setup_quote = 100 }
	set_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_security_setup_quote = gdp_total }
	multiply_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_security_setup_quote = 0.0005 }
	divide_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_security_setup_quote = 100 }
	set_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_regional_setup_quote = gdp_total }
	multiply_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_regional_setup_quote = 0.0005 }
	divide_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_regional_setup_quote = 100 }
	set_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_signal_quote = gdp_total }
	multiply_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_signal_quote = 0.0002 }
	divide_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_signal_quote = 100 }
}

COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_exchange_expire = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = USA
			has_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_exchange_signed
			check_variable = { var = global.num_days value = COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_exchange_end compare = greater_than_or_equals }
		}
		COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_exchange_end = yes
	}
}

COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_reconcile = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = USA
			IMP_is_1970_scenario = yes
		}
		COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_refresh_quotes = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_exchange_signed
				check_variable = { var = global.num_days value = COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_exchange_end compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			}
			COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_exchange_end = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_exchange_active = yes
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { has_idea = COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_exchange_usa }
				}
				add_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_exchange_usa
			}
			CHI = { if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { has_idea = COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_exchange_china }
				}
				add_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_exchange_china
			} }
		}
		else = {
			remove_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_exchange_usa
			CHI = { remove_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_exchange_china }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_industry_active = yes
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { has_idea = COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_industry_usa }
				}
				add_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_industry_usa
			}
			CHI = { if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { has_idea = COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_industry_china }
				}
				add_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_industry_china
			} }
		}
		else = {
			remove_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_industry_usa
			CHI = { remove_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_industry_china }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_security_active = yes
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { has_idea = COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_security_usa }
				}
				add_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_security_usa
			}
			CHI = { if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { has_idea = COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_security_china }
				}
				add_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_security_china
			} }
		}
		else = {
			remove_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_security_usa
			CHI = { remove_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_security_china }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_regional_active = yes
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { has_idea = COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_regional_usa }
				}
				add_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_regional_usa
			}
			CHI = { if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { has_idea = COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_regional_china }
				}
				add_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_regional_china
			} }
		}
		else = {
			remove_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_regional_usa
			CHI = { remove_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_regional_china }
		}
	}
}

COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_missing_usa_cleanup = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = CHI
			NOT = { country_exists = USA }
		}
		remove_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_exchange_china
		remove_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_industry_china
		remove_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_security_china
		remove_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_regional_china
	}
}

COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_streams = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = { tag = USA tag = CHI }
		}
		COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_missing_usa_cleanup = yes
		if = { limit = { country_exists = USA } USA = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_reconcile = yes } }
		if = {
			limit = {
				tag = USA
				COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_exchange_active = yes
			}
			set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_weekly_amount = gdp_total }
			multiply_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_weekly_amount = 0.00002 }
			divide_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_weekly_amount = 100 }
			add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_weekly_expense = COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_weekly_amount }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				tag = CHI
				USA = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_exchange_active = yes }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_weekly_amount = gdp_total }
			multiply_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_weekly_amount = 0.0002 }
			divide_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_weekly_amount = 100 }
			add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_weekly_expense = COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_weekly_amount }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				tag = USA
				COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_industry_active = yes
			}
			set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_weekly_amount = gdp_total }
			multiply_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_weekly_amount = 0.00005 }
			divide_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_weekly_amount = 100 }
			add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_weekly_expense = COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_weekly_amount }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				tag = CHI
				USA = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_industry_active = yes }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_weekly_amount = USA.gdp_total }
			multiply_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_weekly_amount = 0.00005 }
			divide_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_weekly_amount = 100 }
			add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_weekly_income = COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_weekly_amount }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				tag = USA
				COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_security_active = yes
			}
			set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_weekly_amount = gdp_total }
			multiply_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_weekly_amount = 0.00004 }
			divide_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_weekly_amount = 100 }
			add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_weekly_expense = COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_weekly_amount }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				tag = CHI
				USA = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_security_active = yes }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_weekly_amount = gdp_total }
			multiply_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_weekly_amount = 0.0004 }
			divide_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_weekly_amount = 100 }
			add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_weekly_expense = COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_weekly_amount }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				tag = USA
				COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_regional_active = yes
			}
			set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_weekly_amount = gdp_total }
			multiply_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_weekly_amount = 0.00005 }
			divide_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_weekly_amount = 100 }
			add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_weekly_expense = COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_weekly_amount }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				tag = CHI
				USA = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_regional_active = yes }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_weekly_amount = USA.gdp_total }
			multiply_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_weekly_amount = 0.00005 }
			divide_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_weekly_amount = 100 }
			add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_weekly_income = COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_weekly_amount }

		# IMPERIA COLD WAR — one treasury line like MD (24.11.2026)

COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_signal_accept = {
	USA = { set_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_signal_quote = gdp_total }
	multiply_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_signal_quote = 0.0002 }
	divide_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_signal_quote = 100 } }
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_signal_accept_valid = yes
			USA = { check_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_signal_quote > 0 } check_variable = { var = treasury value = COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_signal_quote compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
		}
		USA = { set_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_signal_signed clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_signal_pending set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = gdp_total }
		multiply_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -0.0002 }
		divide_temp_variable = { treasury_change = 100 }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes add_timed_idea = { idea = COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_signal_usa days = 180 } news_event = { id = COLDWAR_USA_TD.101 } }
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_signal_signed
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_signal_pending
	}
}

COLDWAR_USA_KS_deal_moscow_end = {
	USA = {
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_deal_moscow_pending
	}
	SOV = {
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_deal_moscow_pending
	}
}

COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_migrate_moscow = {
	USA = {
		remove_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_deal_moscow_usa
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_deal_moscow_signed
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_deal_moscow_pending
	}
	SOV = {
		remove_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_deal_moscow_partner
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_deal_moscow_signed
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_deal_moscow_pending
	}
}

COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_reconcile_priorities = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_USA_KS_usa_actor = yes
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_priority_weekly = gdp_total }
		multiply_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_priority_weekly = 0.00002 }
		divide_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_priority_weekly = 100 }
		if = {
			limit = {
				COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_priority_china_active = yes
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { has_idea = COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_priority_china }
				}
				add_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_priority_china
			}
		}
		else = {
			remove_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_priority_china
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_priority_moscow_active = yes
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { has_idea = COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_priority_moscow }
				}
				add_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_priority_moscow
			}
		}
		else = {
			remove_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_priority_moscow
		}
	}
}

COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_strategy_streams = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = USA
		}
		COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_reconcile_priorities = yes
		COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_sov_signal_quote = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_priority_china_active = yes
			}
			add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_weekly_expense = COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_priority_weekly }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_priority_moscow_active = yes
			}
			add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_weekly_expense = COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_priority_weekly }
		}
	}
}

COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_sov_signal_quote = {
	set_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_statement_quote = gdp_total }
	multiply_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_statement_quote = 0.0002 }
	divide_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_statement_quote = 100 }
}

COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_sov_signal_preview = {
	effect_tooltip = {
		custom_effect_tooltip = COLDWAR_USA_KS_if_accept_tt
		USA = {
			add_timed_idea = { idea = COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_sov_signal_spirit days = 180 }
			add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_preview_foreign_influence_modifier = 0.03 tooltip = COLDWAR_USA_KS_mod_foreign_influence_modifier_tt }
			# IMPERIA COLD WAR — one treasury line like MD (24.11.2026)
			set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = gdp_total }
			multiply_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -0.0002 }
			divide_temp_variable = { treasury_change = 100 }
			modify_treasury_effect = yes
		}
	}
}

COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_sov_signal_offer = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_sov_signal_can_offer = yes
		}
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_statement_quote = gdp_total }
		multiply_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_statement_quote = 0.0002 }
		divide_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_statement_quote = 100 }
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_sov_signal_pending
		SOV = {
			set_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_sov_signal_pending
			country_event = { id = COLDWAR_USA_TDSP.1 }
		}
	}
}

COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_sov_signal_accept = {
	USA = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_sov_signal_quote = yes }
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_sov_signal_accept_valid = yes
		}
		USA = {
			set_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_sov_signal_completed
			set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = gdp_total }
			multiply_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -0.0002 }
			divide_temp_variable = { treasury_change = 100 }
			modify_treasury_effect = yes
			add_timed_idea = { idea = COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_sov_signal_spirit days = 180 }
			update_money_dirty_variable = yes
		}
		USA = {
			clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_sov_signal_pending
		}
		SOV = {
			clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_sov_signal_pending
		}
		news_event = { id = COLDWAR_USA_TDSP.2 }
	}
}

COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_sov_signal_end = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = { tag = USA tag = SOV }
		}
		USA = {
			clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_sov_signal_pending
		}
		SOV = {
			clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_sov_signal_pending
		}
		USA = { set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_sov_signal_cooldown days = 30 } }
	}
}

COLDWAR_USA_KS_regional_research = {
	set_technology = {
		popup = no
		mdcw_infantry_weapons_1940 = 1
		mdcw_util_vehicle_1940 = 1
		mdcw_cnc_equipment_1940 = 1
		mdcw_support_weapons_1940 = 1
		mdcw_special_forces_1940 = 1
		mdcw_camouflage_1940 = 1
		mdcw_body_armor_1945 = 1
		mdcw_anti_tank_1940 = 1
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_dlc = "No Step Back"
		}
		set_technology = {
			popup = no
			mdcw_nsb_anti_air_1940 = 1
		}
	}
	else = {
		set_technology = {
			popup = no
			mdcw_anti_air_1940 = 1
		}
	}
}

COLDWAR_USA_KS_initialize_new_actor = {
	set_variable = { treasury = 0 }
	set_variable = { debt = 0 }
	set_variable = { int_investments = 0 }
	set_variable = { nation_to_copy_from = 0 }
	set_variable = { corporate_tax_rate = 20 }
	set_variable = { population_tax_rate = 20 }
	set_variable = { domestic_influence_amount = 100 }
	set_popularities = { neutrality = 100 }
	set_politics = { ruling_party = neutrality elections_allowed = no }
	start_politics_input = yes
	set_variable = { party_pop_array^18 = 1 }
	set_variable = { ruling_party = 18 }
	startup_politics = yes
	setup_employment_variables = yes
	set_law_vars = yes
	currency_startup = yes
	ingame_update_setup = yes
	set_variable = { gdpc_converging_var = gdp_per_capita }
	init_auto_influence_array = yes
	ingame_init_investment_system = yes
	research_slot_init_for_country = yes
	setup_init_factions = yes
	set_variable = { last_quarter_gdp_total = gdp_total }
	set_variable = { last_quarter_gdpc_total = gdp_per_capita }
	COLDWAR_USA_KS_regional_research = yes
	set_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_regional_actor
	if = { limit = { NOT = { is_in_array = { array = global.Unrecognised_States value = THIS } } } add_to_array = { array = global.Unrecognised_States value = THIS } }
}

COLDWAR_USA_KS_calculate_streams = {
	COLDWAR_USA_KS_refresh_fiscal = yes
	set_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_weekly_expense = 0 }
	set_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_weekly_income = 0 }
	if = {
		limit = {
			IMP_is_1970_scenario = yes
		}
		COLDWAR_USA_KS_deal_streams = yes
		COLDWAR_USA_KS_regional_streams = yes
		COLDWAR_USA_KS_domestic_streams = yes
		COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_streams = yes
		COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_strategy_streams = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				tag = USA
				has_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_chile_funding
				country_exists = CHL
			}
			add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_weekly_expense = COLDWAR_USA_KS_week_us_chile }
		}
	}
}
